$c$ ,  $u$ ,  $\partial e$ ,  $u^{\mathrm{tmp}}$ ,  $\gamma^{\mathrm{tmp}}$ ,  $r_1$ ,  $r_2$ ,  $r_3$ ,  $r_4$ . finally, query (algorithm 12) outputs  $r$ . the goal of this section is to prove lemma d.7 which gives a close-form formula of the output  $r$ .

lemma d.7 (correctness of query). before entering query (algorithm 12), if we have the following two guarantees:  $|s \cup \partial s| \leq 2n^a$ , and all the invariants of assumption d.1 are satisfied, then the output  $r$  is

$$
r = r [ l ] ^ {\top} r [ l ] \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} m ^ {\mathrm {n e w}} \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}),
$$

where  $m^{\mathrm{new}} = a^{\top}(aw^{\mathrm{app}}a^{\top})^{-1}a$

this lemma is proved in claim d.12 using the following:

1.  $r_1 = q[l]\sqrt{v} f(g)$  (claim d.8)  
2.  $r_2 = (q[l] + r[l](\gamma + \partial \gamma)m) \cdot (\sqrt{w^{\mathrm{app}}} f(h^{\mathrm{app}}) - \sqrt{v} f(g))$  (claim d.9)  
3.  $r_3 = r[l](\gamma + \partial \gamma)m\sqrt{v} f(g)$  (claim d.10)  
4.  $r_4 = -r[l]\sqrt{w^{\mathrm{app}}}$ $m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}}((\delta_{s^{\mathrm{new}},s^{\mathrm{new}}}^{\mathrm{new}})^{-1} + m_{s^{\mathrm{new}},s^{\mathrm{new}}})^{-1}(m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}})^\top \sqrt{w^{\mathrm{app}}}$ $f(h^{\mathrm{app}})$  (claim d.11)

now we prove these claims one by one. in the following we assume that  $|s \cup \partial s| \leq 2n^a$  and all the invariants of assumption d.1 are satisfied. note that when  $|s \cup \partial s| \leq 2n^a$ , all of the  $\mathcal{l}, \mathcal{l}_c, \mathcal{l}_r,$  and  $\mathcal{l}_*$  are well-defined, and the decompose function is also well-defined.

claim d.8 (close-form formula for  $r_1$ ). we have  $r_1 = q[l]\sqrt{v} f(g)$ .

proof. from the assignment of  $r_1$  (line 5 of algorithm 12), we have

$$
r _ {1} = \beta_ {1} [ l ] = q [ l ] \sqrt {v} f (g).
$$

where the last step follows from  $\beta_{1} = q\sqrt{v} f(g)$  (part 2 of assumption d.1).

claim d.9 (close-form formula for  $r_2$ ). we have

$$
r _ {2} = (q [ l ] + r [ l ] (\gamma + \partial \gamma) m) \cdot (\sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}) - \sqrt {v} f (g)).
$$

proof. from the assignment of  $r_2$  (line 6 of algorithm 12), we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} r _ {2} = q [ l ] \xi + r [ l ] \gamma_ {2} + r [ l ] \partial \gamma m (\xi + \partial \xi) + (q [ l ] + r [ l ] \gamma m) \partial \xi \\ = (q [ l ] + r [ l ] \gamma m) \xi + r [ l ] \partial \gamma m (\xi + \partial \xi) + (q [ l ] + r [ l ] \gamma m) \partial \xi \\ = \left(q [ l ] + r [ l ] (\gamma + \partial \gamma) m\right) \cdot (\xi + \partial \xi) \\ = \left(q [ l ] + r [ l ] (\gamma + \partial \gamma) m\right) \cdot \left(\sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}) - \sqrt {v} f (g)\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

where the second step follows from  $\gamma_{2} = \gamma m\cdot \xi$  (part 14 of assumption d.1), the third step follows from merging terms, and the fourth step follows from the invariant  $\xi = \sqrt{\widetilde{v}} f(\widetilde{g}) - \sqrt{v} f(g)$  (part 9 of assumption d.1) and the definition  $\partial \xi = \sqrt{w^{\mathrm{app}}} f(h^{\mathrm{app}}) - \sqrt{\widetilde{v}} f(\widetilde{g})$  (part 3 of definition d.2).

claim d.10 (close-form formula for  $r_3$ ). we have  $r_3 = r[l](\gamma + \partial \gamma)m\sqrt{v} f(g)$ .

proof. from the assignment of  $r_3$  (line 7 of algorithm 12), we have

$$
r _ {3} = r [ l ] (\gamma + \partial \gamma) \beta_ {2} = r [ l ] (\gamma + \partial \gamma) m \sqrt {v} f (g),
$$

where the second step follows from the invariant  $\beta_{2} = m\sqrt{v} f(g)$  (part 4 of assumption d.1).

claim d.11 (close-form formula for  $r_4$ ). we have

$$
r _ {4} = - r [ l ] \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} \cdot ((\delta_ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}, s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}) ^ {- 1} + m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}, s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}}) ^ {- 1} \cdot (m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}}) ^ {\top} \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}).
$$

proof. first note that the left part of  $r_4$  is

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathcal {l} _ {c} [ (q [ l ]) _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} ] + f [ l ] + r [ l ] \gamma \cdot (\mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {\partial s \backslash s} ] - \mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {s ^ {\prime}} ]) + r [ l ] \partial \gamma \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} ] \\ = \mathcal {l} _ {c} \left[ \left(q [ l ]\right) _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}} \right] + r [ l ] \gamma \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {c} \left[ m _ {s} \right] + r [ l ] \gamma \cdot \left(\mathcal {l} _ {c} \left[ m _ {\partial s \backslash s} \right] - \mathcal {l} _ {c} \left[ m _ {s ^ {\prime}} \right]\right) + r [ l ] \partial \gamma \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {c} \left[ m _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}} \right] \\ = \mathcal {l} _ {c} \left[ (q [ l ]) _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}} \right] + r [ l ] \gamma \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {c} \left[ m _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}} \right] + r [ l ] \partial \gamma \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {c} \left[ m _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}} \right] \\ = \mathcal {l} _ {c} \left[ (q [ l ]) _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}} \right] + r [ l ] (\gamma + \partial \gamma) \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {c} \left[ m _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}} \right] \\ = r [ l ] \sqrt {v} \mathcal {l} _ {c} \left[ m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} \right] + r [ l ] \left(\gamma + \partial \gamma\right) \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {c} \left[ m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} \right] \\ = r [ l ] \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} \mathcal {l} _ {c} \left[ m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} \right], \tag {37} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first step follows from  $f = r\gamma \cdot \mathcal{l}_c[m_s]$  (part 12 of assumption d.1), the second step follows from  $s' = (s \cup \partial s) \backslash s^{\mathrm{new}}$  (part 9 of definition d.2) and thus  $\mathcal{l}_c[m_{s'}] + \mathcal{l}_c[m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}}] = \mathcal{l}_c[m_{s \cup \partial s}] = \mathcal{l}_c[m_s] + \mathcal{l}_c[m_{\partial s \backslash s}]$  by part 2 of remark c.3, the fourth step follows from  $q = r\sqrt{v} m$  (part 2 of assumption d.1) and part 3 of remark c.3, and the fifth step follows from  $\gamma = \sqrt{\widetilde{v}} - \sqrt{v}$  (part 8 of assumption d.1) and  $\partial \gamma = \sqrt{w^{\mathrm{appr}}} - \sqrt{\widetilde{v}}$  (part 2 of assumption d.2).

we also have

$$
\gamma_ {1} + \partial \gamma = b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (\beta_ {2}) _ {s} ] + b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {s}) ^ {\top} ] \cdot \xi +
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} b \cdot (\mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (\beta_ {2}) _ {\partial s \backslash s} ] - \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (\beta_ {2}) _ {s ^ {\prime}} ]) + b \cdot (\mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {\partial s \backslash s}) ^ {\top} ] - \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {s ^ {\prime}}) ^ {\top} ]) \cdot (\xi + \partial \xi) + e \cdot \partial \xi \\ = \underbrace {b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (\beta_ {2}) _ {s} ] + b \cdot (\mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (\beta_ {2}) _ {\partial s \backslash s} ] - \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (\beta_ {2}) _ {s ^ {\prime}} ])} _ {a _ {1}} + \\ \underbrace {b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \left(m _ {s}\right) ^ {\top} \right] \cdot \xi + b \cdot \left(\mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \left(m _ {\partial s \backslash s}\right) ^ {\top} \right] - \mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \left(m _ {s ^ {\prime}}\right) ^ {\top} \right]\right) \cdot (\xi + \partial \xi) + e \cdot \partial \xi} _ {a _ {2}}, \tag {38} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first step follows from  $\gamma_{1} = b\cdot \mathcal{l}_{r}[(\beta_{2})_{s}] + b\cdot \mathcal{l}_{r}[(m_{s})^{\top}]\cdot \xi$  (part 13 of assumption d.1) and the assignment of  $\partial \gamma$  on line 8 of algorithm 12, the second step follows from changing the order of terms. and

$$
a _ {1} = b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \left(\beta_ {2}\right) _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}} \right], \tag {39}
$$

which follows from  $s' = (s \cup \partial s) \backslash s^{\mathrm{new}}$  (part 9 of definition d.2) and thus  $\mathcal{l}_c[(\beta_2)_{s'}] + \mathcal{l}_c[(\beta_2)_{s^{\mathrm{new}}}] = \mathcal{l}_c[(\beta_2)_{s \cup \partial s}] = \mathcal{l}_c[(\beta_2)_s] + \mathcal{l}_c[(\beta_2)_{\partial s \setminus s}]$  by part 2 of remark c.3. and also

$$
\begin{array}{l} a _ {2} = b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {s}) ^ {\top} ] \cdot \xi + b \cdot (\mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {\partial s \backslash s}) ^ {\top} ] - \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {s ^ {\prime}}) ^ {\top} ]) \cdot (\xi + \partial \xi) + b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {s}) ^ {\top} ] \cdot \partial \xi \\ = b \cdot \left(\mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \left(m _ {s}\right) ^ {\top} \right] + \mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \left(m _ {\partial s \backslash s}\right) ^ {\top} \right] - \mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \left(m _ {s ^ {\prime}}\right) ^ {\top} \right]\right) \cdot (\xi + \partial \xi) \\ = b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \left(m _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}}\right) ^ {\top} \right] \cdot (\xi + \partial \xi), \tag {40} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first step follows from  $e = b \cdot \mathcal{l}_r[(m_s)^\top]$ , the second step follows from merging terms, and the third step follows from  $s' = (s \cup \partial s) \setminus s^{\mathrm{new}}$  (part 9 of definition d.2) and thus  $\mathcal{l}_r[(m_{s'})^\top] + \mathcal{l}_r[(m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}})^\top] = \mathcal{l}_r[(m_{s \cup \partial s})^\top] = \mathcal{l}_r[(m_s)^\top] + \mathcal{l}_r[(m_{\partial s \setminus s})^\top]$  by part 2 of remark c.3.

combining eq. (38), (39), and (40) together, we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \gamma_ {1} + \partial \gamma = b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (\beta_ {2}) _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} ] + b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}}) ^ {\top} ] \cdot (\xi + \partial \xi) \\ = b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}}) ^ {\top} ] \sqrt {v} f (g) + b \cdot l _ {r} [ (m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}}) ^ {\top} ] \cdot (\sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}) - \sqrt {v} f (g)) \\ = b \cdot l _ {r} \left[ \left(m _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}}\right) ^ {\top} \right] \cdot \sqrt {w ^ {\text {a p p r}}} f \left(h ^ {\text {a p p r}}\right), \tag {41} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the second step follows from  $\beta_{2} = m\sqrt{v} f(g)$  (part 4 of assumption d.1) and using part 3 of remark c.3, and  $\xi + \partial \xi = \sqrt{w^{\mathrm{app}}} f(h^{\mathrm{app}}) - \sqrt{v} f(g)$  (part 9 of assumption d.1 and part 3 of definition d.2), and the third step follows from merging terms.

therefore,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \gamma^ {\mathrm {t m p}} - \gamma_ {1} - \partial \gamma = - (i - u ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} (c ^ {- 1} + u ^ {\top} u ^ {\mathrm {t m p}}) ^ {- 1} u ^ {\top}) \cdot (\gamma_ {1} + \partial \gamma) \\ = - \left(i - b u ^ {\prime} \left(c ^ {- 1} + u ^ {\top} b u ^ {\prime}\right) ^ {- 1} u ^ {\top}\right) \cdot (\gamma_ {1} + \partial \gamma) \\ = - \left(i - b u ^ {\prime} \left(c ^ {- 1} + u ^ {\top} b u ^ {\prime}\right) ^ {- 1} u ^ {\top}\right) \cdot b \cdot l _ {r} \left[ \left(m _ {s _ {\text {n e w}}}\right) ^ {\top} \right] \cdot \sqrt {w ^ {\text {a p p r}}} f \left(h ^ {\text {a p p r}}\right) \\ = - \mathcal {l} _ {*} [ \left(\left(\delta_ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}, s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}}\right) + m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}, s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}}\right) ^ {- 1} ] \cdot l _ {r} \left[ \left(m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}}\right) ^ {\top} \right] \cdot \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f \left(h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}\right), \tag {42} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first step is by assignment of  $\gamma^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  on line 16 of algorithm 12, the second step is by  $u^{\mathrm{tmp}} = bu'$  (corollary c.7), the third step follows by eq. (41), the fourth step is by  $b - bu(c^{-1} + u^\top bu)^{-1}u^\top b = \mathcal{l}_*[((\delta_{s^{\mathrm{new}}, s^{\mathrm{new}}}^{\mathrm{new}}) + m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}, s^{\mathrm{new}}})^{-1}]$  (lemma c.6),

then from the assignment of  $r_4$  on line 17 of algorithm 12, we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} r _ {4} = \left(\mathcal {l} _ {c} [ (q [ l ]) _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} ] + f [ l ] + r [ l ] \gamma \cdot (\mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {\partial s \backslash s} ] - \mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {s ^ {\prime}} ]) + r [ l ] \partial \gamma \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} ]\right) \cdot (\gamma^ {\mathrm {t m p}} - \gamma_ {1} - \partial \gamma) \\ = r [ l ] \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} \mathcal {l} _ {c} \left[ m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} \right] \cdot \left(\gamma^ {\mathrm {t m p}} - \gamma_ {1} - \partial \gamma\right) \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = - r [ l ] \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} \mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} ] \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {*} [ ((\delta_ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}, s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}}) ^ {\mathrm {n e w}} + m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}, s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}}) ^ {- 1} ] \cdot l _ {r} [ (m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}}) ^ {\top} ] \cdot \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}) \\ = - r [ l ] \sqrt {w ^ {\text {a p p r}}} m _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}} \cdot \left(\left(\delta_ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}, s ^ {\text {n e w}}}\right) + m _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}, s ^ {\text {n e w}}}\right) ^ {- 1} \cdot \left(m _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}}\right) ^ {\top} \cdot \sqrt {w ^ {\text {a p p r}}} f \left(h ^ {\text {a p p r}}\right), \tag {43} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the second step follows from eq. (37), the third step follows from eq.(42), the fourth step follows from the property of  $\mathcal{l}$  operators (part 4 of remark c.3).

claim d.12 (close-form formula for  $r$ ).

$$
r = r [ l ] ^ {\top} r [ l ] \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} m ^ {\mathrm {n e w}} \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}),
$$

where  $m^{\mathrm{new}} = a^{\top}(aw^{\mathrm{app}}a^{\top})^{-1}a$

proof. from claim d.8, we have  $r_1 = q[l]\sqrt{v} f(g)$ .

from claim d.9, we have  $r_2 = (q[l] + r[l](\gamma + \partial \gamma)m) \cdot (\sqrt{w^{\mathrm{appr}}} f(h^{\mathrm{appr}}) - \sqrt{v} f(g))$ .

from claim d.10, we have  $r_3 = r[l](\gamma + \partial \gamma)m\sqrt{v} f(g)$ .

from claim d.11, we have

$$
r _ {4} = - r [ l ] \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} \cdot ((\delta_ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}, s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}) ^ {- 1} + m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}, s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}}) ^ {- 1} \cdot (m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}}) ^ {\top} \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}).
$$

the proof sketch is as follows: we first compute  $r_1 + r_3$ , then compute  $(r_1 + r_3) + r_2$ , and finally we compute  $(r_1 + r_2 + r_3) + r_4$ . first we compute  $r_1 + r_3$  as follows:

$$
\begin{array}{l} r _ {1} + r _ {3} = q [ l ] \sqrt {v} f (g) + r [ l ] (\gamma + \partial \gamma) m \sqrt {v} f (g) = r [ l ] \sqrt {v} m \sqrt {v} f (g) + r [ l ] (\gamma + \partial \gamma) m \sqrt {v} f (g) \\ = r [ l ] (\sqrt {v} + (\gamma + \partial \gamma)) m \sqrt {v} f (g) = r [ l ] \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} m \sqrt {v} f (g), \tag {44} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first step follows from claim d.8 and d.10, the second step follows from  $q = r\sqrt{v} m$  (part 2 of assumption d.1), the third step follows from merging terms, and the fourth step follows from  $\gamma + \partial \gamma = (\sqrt{\widetilde{v}} - \sqrt{v}) + (\sqrt{w^{\mathrm{appr}}} - \sqrt{\widetilde{v}}) = \sqrt{w^{\mathrm{appr}}} - \sqrt{v}$ , (γ from part 8 in assumption d.1,  $\partial \gamma$  from part 2 in definition d.2).

secondly, we can compute  $(r_1 + r_3) + r_2$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(r _ {1} + r _ {3}\right) + r _ {2} = r [ l ] \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} m \sqrt {v} f (g) + \left(q [ l ] + r [ l ] (\gamma + \partial \gamma) m\right) \left(\sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f \left(h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}\right) - \sqrt {v} f (g)\right) \\ = r [ l ] \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} m \sqrt {v} f (g) + (r [ l ] \sqrt {v} m + r [ l ] (\gamma + \partial \gamma) m) (\sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}) - \sqrt {v} f (g)) \\ = r [ l ] \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} m \sqrt {v} f (g) + r [ l ] \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} m (\sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}) - \sqrt {v} f (g)) \\ = r [ l ] \sqrt {w ^ {\text {a p p r}}} m \sqrt {w ^ {\text {a p p r}}} f \left(h ^ {\text {a p p r}}\right), \tag {45} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first step follows from eq. (44) and claim d.9, the second step follows from  $q = r\sqrt{v} m$  (part 2 of assumption d.1), the third step follows from  $\gamma + \partial \gamma = (\sqrt{\widetilde{v}} - \sqrt{v}) + (\sqrt{w^{\mathrm{app}}} - \sqrt{\widetilde{v}}) = \sqrt{w^{\mathrm{app}}} - \sqrt{v}$  ( $\gamma$  from part 8 in assumption d.1,  $\partial \gamma$  from part 2 in definition d.2), and the fourth step follows from merging terms.

finally, we can compute  $r_1 + r_2 + r_3 + r_4$ .

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(r _ {1} + r _ {2} + r _ {3}\right) + r _ {4} = r [ l ] \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} m \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f \left(h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}\right) - \\ r [ l ] \sqrt {w ^ {\text {a p p r}}} m _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}} \left(\left(\delta_ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}, s ^ {\text {n e w}}} ^ {\text {n e w}}\right) ^ {- 1} + m _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}, s ^ {\text {n e w}}}\right) ^ {- 1} \left(m _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}}\right) ^ {\top} \sqrt {w ^ {\text {a p p r}}} f \left(h ^ {\text {a p p r}}\right) \\ = r [ l ] \sqrt {w ^ {\text {a p p r}}} \left(m - m _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}} \left(\left(\delta_ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}, s ^ {\text {n e w}}}\right) ^ {- 1} + m _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}, s ^ {\text {n e w}}}\right) ^ {- 1} \left(m _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}}\right) ^ {\top}\right) \sqrt {w ^ {\text {a p p r}}} f \left(h ^ {\text {a p p r}}\right) \\ = r [ l ] \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} m ^ {\mathrm {n e w}} \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f \left(h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}\right), \tag {46} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first step follows from eq. (45) and claim d.11, the second step follows from merging terms, and the third step follows from lemma c.8 (by setting the parameters in the lemma statement as  $\delta \gets \delta^{\mathrm{new}}$ ,  $s \gets s^{\mathrm{new}}$ ,  $\widetilde{v} \gets w^{\mathrm{app}}$ ) and the definition that  $m^{\mathrm{new}} = a^{\top}(aw^{\mathrm{app}}a^{\top})^{-1}a$ .

therefore, from the assignment of  $r$  on line 18 of algorithm 12, we have

$$
r = r [ l ] ^ {\top} (r _ {1} + r _ {2} + r _ {3} + r _ {4}) = r [ l ] ^ {\top} r [ l ] \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} m ^ {\mathrm {n e w}} \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}).
$$

# d.2 correctness of updatev and updateg

lemma d.13 (correctness of updatev). after executing the procedure updatev, the following properties are satisfied:

1.  $\| w^{\mathrm{app}} - v\| _0\leq n^a$  ,  $\| w^{\mathrm{app}} - \widetilde{v}\| _0\leq n^{\widetilde{a}}$  
2. if all invariants of assumption d.1 are satisfied before entering the procedure updatev (algorithm 9), then all invariants are still satisfied after updatev.

proof. part 1. the procedure updatev could exit in three places: line 22, 19 and 12. we discuss them case by case.

(a. line 22).  $w^{\mathrm{app}}$  is assigned to be  $\widetilde{v}^{\mathrm{new}}$ . the algorithm avoids the if branches on line 6 and the if branch on line 14. therefore, both of the conditions of the if branches are false, so we have  $\| \widetilde{v}^{\mathrm{new}} - v \|_0 < n^a$  and  $\| \widetilde{v}^{\mathrm{new}} - \widetilde{v} \|_0 < n^{\widetilde{a}}$ .  
(b. line 19).  $w^{\mathrm{appr}}$  is assigned to be  $\widetilde{v}^{\mathrm{new}}$ . the algorithm avoids the if branch on line 6, so we have  $\| \widetilde{v}^{\mathrm{new}} - v \|_0 < n^a$ . then the algorithm enters procedure partialmatrixupdate (see line 16) to update  $\widetilde{v} \gets \widetilde{v}^{\mathrm{new}}$  (see line 16 of algorithm 14), so  $\| \widetilde{v}^{\mathrm{new}} - \widetilde{v} \|_0 = 0 < n^{\widetilde{a}}$ .  
(c. line 12).  $w^{\mathrm{app}}$  is assigned to be  $v^{\mathrm{new}}$ . the algorithm enters the procedure matrixupdate (see line 9) to update  $v \gets \widetilde{v} \gets w^{\mathrm{app}}$  (see line 13 of algorithm 13). thus  $\| v^{\mathrm{new}} - v \|_0 = 0 < n^a$  and  $\| v^{\mathrm{new}} - \widetilde{v} \|_0 = 0 < n^{\widetilde{a}}$ .

part 2. we first prove that  $|s \cup \partial s| \leq 2n^a$  is satisfied if we enter partialmatrixupdate. note that the input  $w^{\mathrm{appr}}$  of partialmatrixupdate is  $\widetilde{v}^{\mathrm{new}}$  (line 16), so  $\partial s = \operatorname{supp}(w^{\mathrm{appr}} - \widetilde{v}) = \operatorname{supp}(\widetilde{v}^{\mathrm{new}} - \widetilde{v})$  (part 4 of definition d.2). we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} | s \cup \partial s | = | s | + | \partial s \backslash s | \leq n ^ {a} + | \partial s \backslash s | = n ^ {a} + | \{i \in [ n ]: v _ {i} = \widetilde {v} _ {i}, \widetilde {v} _ {i} ^ {\mathrm {n e w}} \neq \widetilde {v} _ {i} \} | \\ = n ^ {a} + | \{i \in [ n ]: \widetilde {v} _ {i} ^ {\mathrm {n e w}} \neq v _ {i} \} | \leq 2 n ^ {a}, \\ \end{array}
$$

where the second step follows from  $|s| \leq n^a$  which is a direct implication of part 1 of this lemma (see proof of corollary d.15), the third step follows from  $s = \mathrm{supp}(v - \widetilde{v})$  (part 5 of assumption d.1) and  $\partial s = \mathrm{supp}(\widetilde{v}^{\mathrm{new}} - \widetilde{v})$ , and the fifth step follows from  $|\mathrm{supp}(\widetilde{v}^{\mathrm{new}} - v)| < n^a$  since the if-clause of line 6 of updatev (algorithm 9) has to be false to enter partialmatrixupdate.

in procedure updatev, the data structure members are modified only by procedure matrixupdate (on line 9) and procedure partialmatrixupdate (on line 16). since all invariants are satisfied before entering matrixupdate or partialmatrixupdate, and  $|s \cup \partial s| \leq 2n^a$  is also satisfied before entering partialmatrixupdate, from lemma d.17 and lemma d.21 we know that all the invariants are still satisfied after matrixupdate and partialmatrixupdate.

lemma d.14 (correctness of updateg). after executing the procedure updateg, the following properties are satisfied:

1.  $\| h^{\mathrm{appr}} - g\| _0\leq n^a$  ,  $\| h^{\mathrm{appr}} - \widetilde{g}\| _0\leq n^{\widetilde{a}}$  
2. if all invariants of assumption d.1 are satisfied before entering the procedure updateg (algorithm 10), then all invariants are still satisfied after updateg.

proof. part 1. the proof is analogous to that of part 1 of lemma d.14.  $\square$

part 2. in procedure updateg, the data structure members are modified only by procedure vectorupdate (see line 9) and procedure partial vectorupdate (see line 15). so this directly follows from the fact that all the invariants are satisfied after vectorupdate (lemma d.25) and partial vectorupdate (lemma d.29).

by the same reasoning, we immediately have the following corollary:

corollary d.15 (sparsity of  $\widetilde{v} - v$  and  $\widetilde{g} - g$ ). throughout the algorithm the following is always satisfied:

$$
\| \widetilde {v} - v \| _ {0} \leq n ^ {a}, \| \widetilde {g} - g \| _ {0} \leq n ^ {a}.
$$

corollary d.16 (sparsity guarantees when entering the procedures). let  $k$  and  $\widetilde{k}$  be the output returned by updatev (line 4 in algorithm 8). let  $p$ ,  $\widetilde{p}$  be the output returned by updateg (line 5 in algorithm 8).

1. when entering the procedure matrixupdate (algorithm 13), we have

$$
\left\| w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}} - v \right\| _ {0} = k.
$$

2. when entering the procedure partialmatrixupdate (algorithm 14), we have

$$
\left\| w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}} - v \right\| _ {0} \leq n ^ {a}, \left\| w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}} - \widetilde {v} \right\| _ {0} = \widetilde {k} \leq 2 n ^ {a}.
$$

3. when entering the procedure vectorupdate (algorithm 15), we have

$$
\| w ^ {\text {a p p r}} - v \| _ {0} \leq n ^ {a}, \| w ^ {\text {a p p r}} - \widetilde {v} \| _ {0} \leq n ^ {\widetilde {a}}, \| h ^ {\text {a p p r}} - g \| _ {0} = p.
$$

4. when entering the procedure partialvectorupdate (algorithm 16), we have

$$
\| w ^ {\text {a p p r}} - v \| _ {0} \leq n ^ {a}, \| w ^ {\text {a p p r}} - \widetilde {v} \| _ {0} \leq n ^ {\widetilde {a}}, \| h ^ {\text {a p p r}} - g \| _ {0} \leq n ^ {a}, \| h ^ {\text {a p p r}} - \widetilde {g} \| _ {0} = \widetilde {p} \leq 2 n ^ {a}.
$$

5. when entering the procedure query (algorithm 12), we have

$$
\| w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}} - v \| _ {0} \leq n ^ {a}, \| w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}} - \widetilde {v} \| _ {0} \leq n ^ {\widetilde {a}}, \| h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}} - g \| _ {0} \leq n ^ {a}, \| h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}} - \widetilde {g} \| _ {0} \leq n ^ {\widetilde {a}}.
$$

proof. all line number mentioned in the proof is in updatev (algorithm 9).

part 1. matrixupdate is entered in line 9, and its input  $w^{\mathrm{appr}}$  is  $v^{\mathrm{new}}$ , which is defined on line 7. so  $\| w^{\mathrm{appr}} - v \|_0 = \| v^{\mathrm{new}} - v \|_0 = k$  directly follows from the definition of  $k$ .

part 2. partialmatrixupdate is entered in line 16, and its input  $w^{\mathrm{appr}}$  is  $\widetilde{v}^{\mathrm{new}}$ , which is defined on line 4.  $\| w^{\mathrm{appr}} - v \|_0 \leq n^a$  is because the algorithm bypass the if-branch in line 6. and  $\| w^{\mathrm{appr}} - \widetilde{v} \|_0 = \| \widetilde{v}^{\mathrm{new}} - v \|_0 = \widetilde{k}$  directly follows from the definition of  $\widetilde{k}$ . and  $\| w^{\mathrm{appr}} - \widetilde{v} \|_0 \leq \| w^{\mathrm{appr}} - v \|_0 + \| v - \widetilde{v} \|_0 \leq 2n^a$  follows from triangle inequality and corollary d.15.

part 3.4. the guarantee that  $\| w^{\mathrm{app}} - v\| _0\leq n^a$ $\| w^{\mathrm{app}} - \widetilde{v}\| _0\leq n^{\widetilde{a}}$  is from part 1 of lemma d.13. the remaining proof is the same as part 1 and part 2.

part 5. this directly follows from part 1 of lemma d.13 and part 1 of lemma d.14.

# d.3 correctness of matrixupdate

lemma d.17 (correctness of matrixupdate). if all invariants of assumption d.1 are satisfied before entering the procedure matrixupdate (algorithm 13), then after the procedure matrixupdate we have the following guarantees:

1.  $v = \widetilde{v} = w^{\mathrm{app}}$  
2.  $g$  and  $\widetilde{g}$  both remain the same.

3. all invariants of assumption d.1 are still satisfied.

part 1 and 2 are proved in claim d.18, and part 3 is proved in claim d.20 by using claim d.19.

claim d.18 (part 1 and 2 of lemma d.17). after the procedure matrixupdate (algorithm 13), we have  $v = \widetilde{v} = w^{\mathrm{app}}$ , and  $g$  and  $\widetilde{g}$  remain the same.

proof. this follows directly from the value assignment of  $v$  and  $\widetilde{v}$  on line 13 of algorithm 13, and the fact that  $g$  and  $\widetilde{g}$  are not modified by algorithm 13.

claim d.19. in the procedure matrixupdate (algorithm 13), before we refresh variables in the memory of data structure, we have the following:

1.  $m^{\mathrm{tmp}} = a^{\top}(aw^{\mathrm{app}}a^{\top})^{-1}a$ ,  
2.  $q^{\mathrm{tmp}} = r\sqrt{w^{\mathrm{appr}}} m^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  
3.  $\beta_{1}^{\mathrm{tmp}} = q^{\mathrm{tmp}}\sqrt{w^{\mathrm{appr}}} f(g)$  
4.  $\beta_{2}^{\mathrm{tmp}} = m^{\mathrm{new}}\sqrt{w^{\mathrm{appr}}} f(g)$  
5.  $\xi^{\mathrm{tmp}} = \sqrt{w^{\mathrm{appr}}} f(\widetilde{g}) - \sqrt{w^{\mathrm{appr}}} f(g)$

proof. part 1. on line 5 of algorithm 13 we assigned  $m^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  as

$$
m ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} = m - m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} \cdot \left(\left(\delta_ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}, s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}\right) ^ {- 1} + m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}, s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}}\right) ^ {- 1} \cdot \left(m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}}\right) ^ {\top}.
$$

since  $m = a^{\top}(ava^{\top})^{-1}a$  (part 1 of assumption d.1),  $\delta^{\mathrm{new}} = w^{\mathrm{appr}} - v$  (part 7 of assumption d.1), and  $s = \operatorname{supp}(w^{\mathrm{appr}} - v)$  (part 5 of assumption d.1), from lemma c.8 we have

$$
m ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} = a ^ {\top} \left(a w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}} a ^ {\top}\right) ^ {- 1} a.
$$

part 2. we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} q ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} = q + r \left(\gamma^ {\mathrm {n e w}} m ^ {\mathrm {t m p}}\right) + r \sqrt {v} \left(m ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} - m\right) = r \sqrt {v} m + r \left(\gamma^ {\mathrm {n e w}} m ^ {\mathrm {t m p}}\right) + r \sqrt {v} \left(m ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} - m\right) \\ = r (\gamma^ {\mathrm {n e w}} + \sqrt {v}) m ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} = r (\gamma + \partial \gamma + \sqrt {v}) m ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \\ = r ((\sqrt {\widetilde {v}} - \sqrt {v}) + (\sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} - \sqrt {\widetilde {v}}) + \sqrt {v}) m ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} = r \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} m ^ {\mathrm {t m p}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first step follows from the assigned value for  $q^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  on line 6 of algorithm 13, the second step follows from  $q = r\sqrt{v} m$  (part 2 of assumption d.1), the third step is by merging terms, the fourth step follows from  $\gamma^{\mathrm{new}} = \gamma + \partial \gamma$  (part 6 of definition d.2), the fifth step follows from  $\gamma = \sqrt{\widetilde{v}} - \sqrt{v}$  (part 8 of assumption d.1) and  $\partial \gamma = \sqrt{w^{\mathrm{appr}}} - \sqrt{\widetilde{v}}$  (part 2 of definition d.2), and the last step is by merging terms.

part 3, 4 and 5. these directly follow from the assignment of  $\beta_1^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  on line 7 of algorithm 13, the assignment of  $\beta_2^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  on line 8, and the assignment of  $\xi^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  on line 9.

claim d.20 (part 3 of lemma d.17). all invariants of assumption d.1 are satisfied after the procedure matrixupdate (algorithm 13).

proof. first note that  $g$ ,  $\widetilde{g}$ , and  $t$  all remain the same after matrixupdate. note that  $v$  and  $\widetilde{v}$  are both assigned the value  $w^{\mathrm{appr}}$  (line 13 of algorithm 13). then using claim d.19, and since we assigned  $m^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  to  $m$ ,  $q^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  to  $q$ ,  $\beta_1^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  to  $\beta_1$ ,  $\beta_2^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  to  $\beta_2$ , and  $\xi^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  to  $\xi$ , we have

$$
m = a ^ {\top} \left(a v a ^ {\top}\right) ^ {- 1} a, \quad q = r \sqrt {v} m,
$$

$$
\beta_ {1} = q \sqrt {v} f (g), \quad \beta_ {2} = m \sqrt {v} f (g),
$$

$$
\xi = \sqrt {\widetilde {v}} f (\widetilde {g}) - \sqrt {v} f (g).
$$

we prove the other invariants directly from the assignment on line 15 of algorithm 13:

$$
s = \emptyset = \operatorname {s u p p} (\widetilde {v} - v), \quad \delta = 0 = \widetilde {v} - v,
$$

$$
\gamma = 0 = \sqrt {\widetilde {v}} - \sqrt {v}, \qquad \qquad \qquad b = i = \mathcal {l} _ {*} [ (\delta_ {s, s} ^ {- 1} + m _ {s, s}) ^ {- 1} ],
$$

$$
\gamma_ {1} = 0 = b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ \beta_ {2, s} ] + b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \left(m _ {s}\right) ^ {\top} \right] \cdot \xi , \quad \gamma_ {2} = 0 = \gamma m \cdot \xi ,
$$

$$
e = 0 = b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {\emptyset}) ^ {\top} ] = b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {s}) ^ {\top} ], \qquad f = 0 = r \gamma \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {\emptyset} ] = r \gamma \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {s} ].
$$

# d.4 correctness of partialmatrixupdate

lemma d.21 (correctness of partialmatrixupdate). if all invariants of assumption d.1 are satisfied before entering the procedure partialmatrixupdate (algorithm 14), and  $|s \cup \partial s| \leq 2n^a$ , then after the procedure partialmatrixupdate we have the following guarantees:

1.  $\widetilde{v} = w^{\mathrm{app}}$ , and  $v$  remains the same.  
2.  $g, \widetilde{g}$  both remain the same.  
3. all invariants of assumption d.1 are still satisfied.

note that since we have the guarantee that  $|s \cup \partial s| \leq 2n^a$ , all  $\mathcal{l}_r, \mathcal{l}_c, \mathcal{l}_*$  operators that appear in the procedure partialmatrixupdate are well-defined. and the decompose function used in partialmatrixupdate is also well-defined.

part 1 and 2 are proved in claim d.22, and part 3 is proved in claim d.24 by using claim d.23.

claim d.22 (part 1 and 2 of lemma d.21). after the procedure partialmatrixupdate (algo-rithm 14), we have  $\widetilde{v} = w^{\mathrm{app}}$ , and  $v, g, \widetilde{g}$  all remain the same.

proof.  $\widetilde{v} = w^{\mathrm{app}}$  follows directly from the value assignment of  $\widetilde{v}$  on line 16 of algorithm 14.

the procedure partialmatrixupdate (algorithm 14) does not modify  $v$ ,  $g$ ,  $\widetilde{g}$ , so they all remain the same.

claim d.23. in the procedure partialmatrixupdate (algorithm 14) before we refresh variables in the memory of the data structure, we have the following:

1.  $b^{\mathrm{tmp}} = \mathcal{l}_*[((\delta_{s^{\mathrm{new}}, s^{\mathrm{new}}}^{\mathrm{new}})^{-1} + m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}, s^{\mathrm{new}}})^{-1}],$  
2.  $\xi^{\mathrm{tmp}} = \sqrt{w^{\mathrm{appr}}} f(\widetilde{g}) - \sqrt{v} f(g)$  
3.  $\gamma_1^{\mathrm{tmp}} = b^{\mathrm{tmp}}\cdot \mathcal{l}_r[\beta_{2,s^{\mathrm{new}}}] + b^{\mathrm{tmp}}\cdot \mathcal{l}_r[(m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}})^\top ]\cdot \xi^{\mathrm{tmp}},$  
4.  $\gamma_2^{\mathrm{tmp}} = \gamma^{\mathrm{new}}m\cdot \xi^{\mathrm{tmp}}$

5.  $f^{\mathrm{tmp}} = r\gamma^{\mathrm{new}}\cdot \mathcal{l}_c[m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}}],$  
6.  $e^{\mathrm{tmp}} = b^{\mathrm{tmp}}\cdot \mathcal{l}_r[(m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}})^\top ]$

proof. part 1. on line 7 of algorithm 14, we assigned  $b^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  as

$$
b ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \leftarrow b - b u ^ {\prime} \left(c ^ {- 1} + u ^ {\top} b u ^ {\prime}\right) ^ {- 1} u ^ {\top} b,
$$

where  $(u', c, u) = \mathrm{decompose}(\mathcal{l}_*[(\delta_{s^{\mathrm{new}}, s^{\mathrm{new}}})^{-1} + m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}, s^{\mathrm{new}}}] - \mathcal{l}_*[\delta_{s, s}^{-1} + m_{s, s}])$ , then using lemma c.6 we have that  $b^{\mathrm{tmp}} = \mathcal{l}_*[((\delta_{s^{\mathrm{new}}, s^{\mathrm{new}}})^{-1} + m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}, s^{\mathrm{new}}})^{-1}]$ .

part 2 and 3. part 2 directly follows from the assignment of  $\xi^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  on line 10 of algorithm 14.

and part 3 directly follows from the assignment of  $\gamma_1^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  on line 11 of algorithm 14.

part 4. we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \gamma_ {2} ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} = \gamma_ {2} + (\gamma + \partial \gamma) m (\sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} - \sqrt {\widetilde {v}}) f (\widetilde {g}) + \partial \gamma m (\sqrt {\widetilde {v}} f (\widetilde {g}) - \sqrt {v} f (g)) \\ = \gamma m \cdot (\sqrt {\widetilde {v}} f (\widetilde {g}) - \sqrt {v} f (g)) + (\gamma + \partial \gamma) m (\sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} - \sqrt {\widetilde {v}}) f (\widetilde {g}) + \partial \gamma m (\sqrt {\widetilde {v}} f (\widetilde {g}) - \sqrt {v} f (g)) \\ = \gamma m \cdot (\sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f (\widetilde {g}) - \sqrt {v} f (g)) + \partial \gamma m (\sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f (\widetilde {g}) - \sqrt {v} f (g)) \\ = (\gamma + \partial \gamma) m \cdot \left(\sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f (\widetilde {g}) - \sqrt {v} f (g)\right) \\ = \gamma^ {\mathrm {n e w}} m \cdot (\sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f (\widetilde {g}) - \sqrt {v} f (g)) \\ = \gamma^ {\mathrm {n e w}} m \cdot \xi^ {\mathrm {t m p}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first step follows from the assignment of  $\gamma_2^{\mathrm{new}}$  (line 12 of algorithm 14), the second step follows from  $\gamma_2 = \gamma m\xi = \gamma m(\sqrt{\widetilde{v}} f(\widetilde{g}) - \sqrt{v} f(g))$  (part 14 and 9 of assumption d.1), the third and the fourth step both follow from merging terms, the fifth step follows from  $\gamma^{\mathrm{new}} = \gamma +\partial \gamma$  (part 2 of definition d.2), and the sixth step follows from the part 2 of this claim.

part 5.

$$
\begin{array}{l} f ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} = f + r \gamma \cdot (\mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {\partial s \backslash s} ] - \mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {s ^ {\prime}} ]) + r \partial \gamma \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} ] \\ = r \gamma \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {s} ] + r \gamma \cdot \left(\mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {\partial s \backslash s} ] - \mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {s ^ {\prime}} ]\right) + r \partial \gamma \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} ] \\ = r \gamma \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {c} \left[ m _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}} \right] + r \partial \gamma \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {c} \left[ m _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}} \right] \\ = r \gamma^ {\text {n e w}} \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {c} \left[ m _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}} \right], \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first step follows from the assignment of  $f^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  (line 8 of algorithm 14), the second step follows from  $f = r\gamma \cdot \mathcal{l}_c[m_s]$  (part 12 of assumption d.1), the third step follows from  $s' = (s \cup \partial s) \backslash s^{\mathrm{new}}$  (part 9 of definition d.2) and thus  $\mathcal{l}_c[m_{s'}] + \mathcal{l}_c[m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}}] = \mathcal{l}_c[m_{s \cup \partial s}] = \mathcal{l}_c[m_s] + \mathcal{l}_c[m_{\partial s \backslash s}]$  by part 2 of remark c.3, and the fourth step follows from  $\gamma^{\mathrm{new}} = \gamma + \partial \gamma$  (part 6 of definition d.2).

part 6

$$
\begin{array}{l} e ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} = e + b ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} (\mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {\partial s \backslash s}) ^ {\top} ] - \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {s ^ {\prime}}) ^ {\top} ]) - b u ^ {\prime} (c ^ {- 1} + u ^ {\top} b u ^ {\prime}) ^ {- 1} u ^ {\top} e \\ = b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {s}) ^ {\top} ] + b ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} (\mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {\partial s \backslash s}) ^ {\top} ] - \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {s ^ {\prime}}) ^ {\top} ]) - b u ^ {\prime} (c ^ {- 1} + u ^ {\top} b u ^ {\prime}) ^ {- 1} u ^ {\top} b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {s}) ^ {\top} ] \\ = \left(b - b u ^ {\prime} (c ^ {- 1} + u ^ {\top} b u ^ {\prime}) ^ {- 1} u ^ {\top} b\right) \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {s}) ^ {\top} ] + b ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} (\mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {\partial s \backslash s}) ^ {\top} ] - \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {s ^ {\prime}}) ^ {\top} ]) \\ = b ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \left(m _ {s}\right) ^ {\top} \right] + b ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \left(\mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \left(m _ {\partial s \backslash s}\right) ^ {\top} \right] - \mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \left(m _ {s ^ {\prime}}\right) ^ {\top} \right]\right) \\ = b ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}}) ^ {\top} ] \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first step follows from the assignment of  $e^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  (line 9 of algorithm 14), the second step follows from  $e = b \cdot \mathcal{l}_r[(m_s)^\top]$  (part 11 of assumption d.1), the fourth step follows from the

definition of  $b^{\mathrm{tmp}}$ , and the fifth step follows from  $s' = (s \cup \partial s) \backslash s^{\mathrm{new}}$  (part 9 of definition d.2) and thus  $\mathcal{l}_r[(m_{s'})^\top] + \mathcal{l}_r[(m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}})^\top] = \mathcal{l}_r[(m_{s \cup \partial s})^\top] = \mathcal{l}_r[(m_s)^\top] + \mathcal{l}_r[(m_{\partial s \setminus s})^\top]$  by part 2 of remark c.3, and the fourth step follows from  $\gamma^{\mathrm{new}} = \gamma + \partial \gamma$  (part 6 of definition d.2).

claim d.24 (part 3 of lemma d.21). all invariants of assumption d.1 are satisfied after the procedure partialmatrixupdate (algorithm 14).

proof. first note that the value of  $v$ ,  $g$ ,  $\widetilde{g}$ ,  $t$ ,  $m$ ,  $q$ ,  $\beta_{1}$  and  $\beta_{2}$  all remain the same after the procedure partialmatrixupdate (algorithm 14). also note that  $\widetilde{v}$  is assigned the value  $w^{\mathrm{app}}$  (line 16 in algorithm 14).

from the assignment of  $s$ ,  $\delta$ , and  $\gamma$  on line 16 of algorithm 14, we have

$$
s = s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}} = \operatorname {s u p p} \left(w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}} - v\right) = \operatorname {s u p p} (\widetilde {v} - v),
$$

$$
\delta = \delta^ {\mathrm {n e w}} = w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}} - v = \widetilde {v} - v,
$$

$$
\gamma = \gamma^ {\mathrm {n e w}} = \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} - \sqrt {v} = \sqrt {\widetilde {v}} - \sqrt {v}.
$$

then using claim d.23, and since we assigned  $b^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  to  $b$ ,  $\xi^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  to  $\xi$ ,  $\gamma_1^{\mathrm{new}}$  to  $\gamma_1$ , and  $\gamma_2^{\mathrm{new}}$  to  $\gamma_2$ ,  $e^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  to  $e$ ,  $f^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  to  $f$ , we have

$$
b = \mathcal {l} _ {*} [ ((\delta_ {s, s}) ^ {- 1} + m _ {s, s}) ^ {- 1} ], \qquad \qquad \xi = \sqrt {\widetilde {v}} f (\widetilde {g}) - \sqrt {v} f (g),
$$

$$
\gamma_ {1} = b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \beta_ {2, s} \right] + b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \left(m _ {s}\right) ^ {\top} \right] \cdot \xi , \quad \gamma_ {2} = \gamma m \cdot \xi ,
$$

$$
e = b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {s}) ^ {\top} ], f = r \gamma \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {s} ].
$$

# d.5 correctness of vectorupdate

lemma d.25 (correctness of vectorupdate). if all invariants of assumption d.1 are satisfied before entering the procedure vectorupdate (algorithm 15), then after the procedure vectorupdate we have the following guarantees:

1.  $v$ ,  $\widetilde{v}$  both remain the same.  
2.  $g = \widetilde{g} = h^{\mathrm{app}}$  
3. all invariants of assumption d.1 are still satisfied.

first note that from corollary d.15 we have that  $|s| \leq n^a$ , so all  $\mathcal{l}_r$  operators that appear in the procedure vectorupdate are well-defined.

part 1 and 2 are proved in claim d.26, and part 3 is proved in claim d.28 by using claim d.27.

claim d.26 (part 1 and 2 of lemma d.25). after the procedure vectorupdate (algorithm 15),  $v$  and  $\widetilde{v}$  both remain the same, and  $g = \widetilde{g} = h^{\mathrm{appr}}$ .

proof. the procedure vectorupdate does not modify  $v$  or  $\widetilde{v}$ , so they both remain the same. and  $g = \widetilde{g} = h^{\mathrm{app}}$  follows directly from the value assignment of  $g$  and  $\widetilde{g}$  on line 11 of algorithm 15.

claim d.27. in the procedure vectorupdate (algorithm 15) before we refresh variables in the memory of the data structure, we have the following:

1.  $\beta_{1}^{\mathrm{tmp}} = q\sqrt{v} f(h^{\mathrm{app}})$  
4.  $\gamma_1^{\mathrm{tmp}} = b\cdot \mathcal{l}_r[\beta_{2,s}^{\mathrm{tmp}}] + b\cdot \mathcal{l}_r[(m_s)^\top ]\cdot \xi^{\mathrm{tmp}},$  
2.  $\beta_{2}^{\mathrm{tmp}} = m\sqrt{v} f(h^{\mathrm{app}})$  
3.  $\xi^{\mathrm{tmp}} = (\sqrt{\widetilde{v}} -\sqrt{v})f(h^{\mathrm{app}})$  
5.  $\gamma_2^{\mathrm{tmp}} = \gamma m\cdot \xi^{\mathrm{tmp}}$

proof. part 1. from the assignment of  $\beta_1^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  on line 4 of algorithm 15, we have

$$
\beta_ {1} ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} = \beta_ {1} + q \sqrt {v} (f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}) - f (g)) = q \sqrt {v} f (g) + q \sqrt {v} (f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}) - f (g)) = q \sqrt {v} f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}),
$$

where the second step follows from  $\beta_{1} = q\sqrt{v} f(g)$  (part 3 of assumption d.1).

part 2. from the assignment of  $\beta_2^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  on line 5 of algorithm 15, we have

$$
\beta_ {2} ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} = \beta_ {2} + m \sqrt {v} (f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}) - f (g)) = m \sqrt {v} f (g) + m \sqrt {v} (f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}) - f (g)) = m \sqrt {v} f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}),
$$

where the second step follows from  $\beta_{2} = m\sqrt{v} f(g)$  (part 4 of assumption d.1).

part 3, 4 and 5. these directly follow from the assignment of  $\xi^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  (line 6),  $\gamma_1^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  (line 7), and  $\gamma_2^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  (line 8) in algorithm 15.

claim d.28 (part 3 of lemma d.25). all invariants of assumption d.1 are satisfied after the procedure vectorupdate (algorithm 15).

proof. first note that  $v, \widetilde{v}, m, q, b, \delta, \gamma$ , and  $s$  all remain the same after the procedure vectorupdate. also note that  $g$  and  $\widetilde{g}$  are both assigned the value  $h^{\mathrm{app}}$  (line 11 of algorithm 15).

then using claim d.27, and since we assigned  $\beta_1^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  to  $\beta_{1}$ ,  $\beta_{2}^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  to  $\beta_{2}$ ,  $\xi^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  to  $\xi$ ,  $\gamma_{1}^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  to  $\gamma_{1}$  and  $\gamma_{2}^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  to  $\gamma_{2}$ , we have

$$
\beta_ {1} = q \sqrt {v} f (g),
$$

$$
\beta_ {2} = m \sqrt {v} f (g),
$$

$$
\gamma_ {1} = b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ \beta_ {2, s} ] + b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \left(m _ {s}\right) ^ {\top} \right] \cdot \xi ,
$$

$$
\gamma_ {2} = \gamma m \cdot \xi ,
$$

$$
\xi = (\sqrt {\widetilde {v}} - \sqrt {v}) f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}) = \sqrt {\widetilde {v}} f (\widetilde {g}) - \sqrt {v} f (g).
$$

also, from the assignment of  $t$  on line 12 of algorithm 15, we have  $t = \emptyset = \operatorname{supp}(\widetilde{g} - g)$ .

# d.6 correctness of partial vector update

lemma d.29 (correctness of partial vector update). if all invariants of assumption d.1 are satisfied before entering the procedure partial vector update (algorithm 16), then after the procedure partial vector update we have the following guarantees:

1.  $v$ ,  $\widetilde{v}$  both remain the same.  
2.  $\widetilde{g} = h^{\mathrm{appr}}$ , and  $g$  remains the same.  
3. all invariants of assumption d.1 are still satisfied.

first note that from corollary d.15 we have that  $|s| \leq n^a$ , so all  $\mathcal{l}_r$  operators that appear in the procedure partial vector update are well-defined.

part 1 and 2 are proved in claim d.30, and part 3 is proved in claim d.32 by using claim d.31.

claim d.30 (part 1 and 2 of lemma d.29). after the procedure partial vector update (algo-rithm 16), we have  $\widetilde{g} = h^{\mathrm{app}}$ , and  $v, \widetilde{v}, g$  all remain the same.

proof.  $\widetilde{g} = h^{\mathrm{app}}$  follows directly from the value assignment of  $\widetilde{g}$  on line 10 of algorithm 16.

the procedure partial vector update (algorithm 16) does not modify  $v$ ,  $\widetilde{v}$ ,  $g$ , so they all remain the same.

claim d.31. in the procedure partial vector update (algorithm 16) before we refresh variables in the memory of the data structure, we have the following:

1.  $\xi^{\mathrm{tmp}} = \sqrt{\widetilde{v}} f(h^{\mathrm{appr}}) - \sqrt{v} f(g)$ ,  
2.  $\gamma_1^{\mathrm{tmp}} = b\cdot \mathcal{l}_r[\beta_{2,s}] + b\cdot \mathcal{l}_r[(m_s)^\top ]\cdot \xi^{\mathrm{tmp}},$  
3.  $\gamma_{2}^{\mathrm{tmp}} = \gamma m\cdot \xi^{\mathrm{tmp}}$

proof. part 1. this directly follows from the assignment of  $\xi^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  on line 4 of algorithm 16.

part 2. from the assignment of  $\gamma_1^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  on line 5 of algorithm 16, we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \gamma_ {1} ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} = \gamma_ {1} + b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \left(m _ {s}\right) ^ {\top} \right] \cdot \sqrt {\widetilde {v}} \left(f \left(h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}\right) - f (\widetilde {g})\right) \\ = b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ \beta_ {2, s} ] + b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {s}) ^ {\top} ] (\sqrt {\widetilde {v}} f (\widetilde {g}) - \sqrt {v} f (g)) + b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {s}) ^ {\top} ] \sqrt {\widetilde {v}} \big (f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}) - f (\widetilde {g}) \big) \\ = b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \beta_ {2, s} \right] + b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \left(m _ {s}\right) ^ {\top} \right] \cdot \left(\left(\sqrt {\widetilde {v}} f (\widetilde {g}) - \sqrt {v} f (g)\right) + \sqrt {\widetilde {v}} \left(f \left(h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}\right) - f (\widetilde {g})\right)\right) \\ = b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ \beta_ {2, s} ] + b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {s}) ^ {\top} ] \cdot (\sqrt {\widetilde {v}} f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}) - \sqrt {v} f (g)) \\ = b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \beta_ {2, s} \right] + b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \left(m _ {s}\right) ^ {\top} \right] \cdot \xi^ {\operatorname {t m p}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

where the second step follows from the invariant of  $\gamma_{1}$  (part 13 in assumption d.1), the third and the fourth steps follow from merging terms, and the last step follows from part 1 of this lemma.

part 3. from the assignment of  $\gamma_2^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  on line 6 of algorithm 16, we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \gamma_ {2} ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} = \gamma_ {2} + \gamma m \sqrt {\widetilde {v}} \big (f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}) - f (\widetilde {g}) \big) = \gamma m (\sqrt {\widetilde {v}} f (\widetilde {g}) - \sqrt {v} f (g)) + \gamma m \sqrt {\widetilde {v}} \big (f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}) - f (\widetilde {g}) \big) \\ = \gamma m (\sqrt {\widetilde {v}} f (h ^ {\text {a p p r}}) - \sqrt {v} f (g)) = \gamma m \cdot \xi^ {\text {t m p}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

where the second step follows from the invariant of  $\gamma_{2}$  (part 14 in assumption d.1), the third step follows from merging terms, and the last step follows from part 1 of this lemma.

claim d.32 (part 3 of lemma d.29). all invariants of assumption d.1 are satisfied after the procedure partial vector update (algorithm 16).

proof. first note that  $v, \widetilde{v}, g, m, q, b, \delta, \gamma, s, \beta_1,$  and  $\beta_2$  all remain the same after the procedure vectorupdate. also note that  $\widetilde{g}$  is assigned the value  $h^{\mathrm{app}}$  (line 10 of algorithm 16).

then using claim d.27, and since we assigned  $\xi^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  to  $\xi$ ,  $\gamma_1^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  to  $\gamma_1$ , and  $\gamma_2^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  to  $\gamma_2$ , we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \gamma_ {1} = b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \beta_ {2, s} \right] + b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} \left[ \left(m _ {s}\right) ^ {\top} \right] \cdot \xi , \quad \gamma_ {2} = \gamma m \cdot \xi , \\ \xi = \sqrt {\widetilde {v}} f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}) - \sqrt {v} f (g) = \sqrt {\widetilde {v}} f (\widetilde {g}) - \sqrt {v} f (g). \\ \end{array}
$$

finally, from the assignment of  $t$  on line 9, we have  $t = \operatorname{supp}(h^{\mathrm{app}} - g) = \operatorname{supp}(\widetilde{g} - g)$ .

# d.7 correctness of initialize

lemma d.33 (correctness of initializer). when initialized, all the invariants of the data structure members stated in assumption d.1 are satisfied.

proof. since in the beginning  $v$  and  $\widetilde{v}$  are both assigned the value  $w_0$ , and  $g$  and  $\widetilde{g}$  are both assigned the value  $h_0$ , it is obvious that  $s = \emptyset$ ,  $t = \emptyset$ ,  $\delta = 0$ ,  $\gamma = 0$ ,  $\xi = 0$ ,  $\gamma_1 = 0$ ,  $\gamma_2 = 0$  all satisfy their invariant requirement of assumption d.1. also,  $b = i = \mathcal{l}_*[0]$  also satisfies the invariant requirement. finally note that the initial assignment of  $m$ ,  $q$ ,  $\beta_1$ ,  $\beta_2$  directly satisfy their invariant requirement of assumption d.1.

# e data structure: time per call

in section e we provide a worst-case analysis of the running time per call for the five major procedures matrixupdate, partialmatrixupdate, vectorupdate, partialvectorupdate, query. we prove the amortized running time of these procedures later in section f. in section e, we ignore the running time of adding two vectors since it is only  $o(n)$ .

lemma e.1 (sparsity guarantees). the members of data structure presented in table 12 and table 13 all follow the invariants of assumption d.1, and the local variables presented in table 13 all follow the definition of definition d.2. we have the following sparsity guarantees.

1. when entering the procedures matrixupdate, partialmatrixupdate, vectorupdate, partial vector update, and query, we have the sparsity bounds on  $\| w^{\mathrm{appr}} - v\| _0$  ,  $\| w^{\mathrm{appr}}-$ $\widetilde{v}\|_{0},\| h^{\mathrm{appr}} - g\|_{0},\| h^{\mathrm{appr}} - \widetilde{g}\|_{0},\| \widetilde{v} -v\|_{0},$  and  $\| \widetilde{g} -g\| _0$  as presented in table 12.

<table><tr><td>procedure</td><td>||δ + ∂δ||0, ||γ + ∂γ||0</td><td>||∂δ||0, ||∂γ||0, |∂s|</td><td>||ξ + ∂ξ||0</td><td>||∂ξ||0</td><td>||δ||0, ||ξ||0, ||γ||0, |s|</td><td>|s∪ ∂s|</td></tr><tr><td>matrixupdate</td><td>=k</td><td>/</td><td>/</td><td>/</td><td>≤na</td><td>≤3k</td></tr><tr><td>p.matriquate</td><td>≤na</td><td>= k ≤ 2na</td><td>/</td><td>/</td><td>≤na</td><td>≤3na</td></tr><tr><td>vectorquate</td><td>≤na</td><td>≤na</td><td>=p</td><td>/</td><td>≤na</td><td>/</td></tr><tr><td>p.vectorquate</td><td>≤na</td><td>≤na</td><td>≤na</td><td>= p ≤ 2na</td><td>≤na</td><td>/</td></tr><tr><td>query</td><td>≤na</td><td>≤na</td><td>≤na</td><td>≤na</td><td>≤na</td><td>≤2na</td></tr></table>

table 13: sparsity guarantees of other local variables (definition d.2) when entering the procedures (part 2 of lemma e.1). we say some vector  $x \in \mathbb{r}^n$  is  $k$ -sparse, it means that  $\operatorname{supp}(x) = k$ .

2. when entering the procedures matrixupdate, partialmatrixupdate, vectorupdate, partialvectorupdate, and query, we have the sparsity bounds on  $\delta +\partial \delta$ $\gamma +\partial \gamma$ $s\cup \partial s$ $\partial \delta$ $\partial \gamma$ $\partial s$ $\xi +\partial \xi$ $\partial \xi$ $\delta$ $\gamma$ $s,\xi$  as presented in table 13.

proof. part 1. the first four columns follow from corollary d.16, and the last two columns follow from corollary d.15.

part 2. for col. 1, we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \delta + \partial \delta \right\| _ {0} = \left\| w ^ {\text {a p p r}} - v \right\| _ {0} = \left\| w ^ {\text {a p p r}} - v \right\| _ {0}, \\ \| \gamma + \partial \gamma \| _ {0} = \| \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} - \sqrt {v} \| _ {0} = \| w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}} - v \| _ {0}, \\ \end{array}
$$

the bounds of this column then follow from col. 1 of table 12.

for col. 2, we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \| \partial \delta \| _ {0} = \| w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}} - \widetilde {v} \| _ {0} = \| w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}} - \widetilde {v} \| _ {0}, \\ \| \partial \gamma \| _ {0} = \| \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} - \sqrt {\widetilde {v}} \| _ {0} = \| w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}} - \widetilde {v} \| _ {0}, \\ | \partial s | = \| w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}} - \widetilde {v} \| _ {0} = \| w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}} - \widetilde {v} \| _ {0}, \\ \end{array}
$$

the bounds of this column then follow from col. 2 of table 12.

for col. 3 we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \| \xi + \partial \xi \| _ {0} = \| \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}) - \sqrt {v} f (g) \| _ {0} \leq \max  \{\| \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} - \sqrt {v} \| _ {0}, \| f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}) - f (g) \| _ {0} \} \\ = \max  \left\{\| w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}} - v \| _ {0}, \| h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}} - g \| _ {0} \right\}, \\ \end{array}
$$

the bounds of this column then follow from col. 1 and col. 3 of table 12 and the fact  $p \geq n^a$  (since we have the equivalent fact for  $p$  as of fact f.10 for  $k$ ).

for col. 4 we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \| \partial \xi \| _ {0} = \| \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}) - \sqrt {\widetilde {v}} f (\widetilde {g}) \| _ {0} \leq \max  \{\| \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} - \sqrt {\widetilde {v}} \| _ {0}, \| f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}) - f (\widetilde {g}) \| _ {0} \} \\ = \max  \left\{\| w ^ {\text {a p p r}} - \widetilde {v} \| _ {0}, \| h ^ {\text {a p p r}} - \widetilde {g} \| _ {0} \right\}, \\ \end{array}
$$

the bounds of this column then follow from col. 2 and col. 4 of table 12 and the fact  $\widetilde{p} \geq n^{\widetilde{a}}$  (since we have the equivalent fact for  $\widetilde{p}$  as of fact f.11 for  $\widetilde{k}$ ).

for col. 5 we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \| \delta \| _ {0} = \| \widetilde {v} - v \| _ {0} = \| \widetilde {v} - v \| _ {0}, \\ \| \gamma \| _ {0} = \| \sqrt {\widetilde {v}} - \sqrt {v} \| _ {0} = \| \widetilde {v} - v \| _ {0}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
| s | = | \operatorname {s u p p} (\widetilde {v} - v) | = \| \widetilde {v} - v \| _ {0},
$$

$$
\| \xi \| _ {0} = \| \sqrt {\widetilde {v}} f (\widetilde {g}) - \sqrt {v} f (g) \| _ {0} \leq \max  \{\| \widetilde {v} - v \| _ {0}, \| \widetilde {g} - g \| _ {0} \},
$$

the bounds of this column then follow from col. 5 and col. 6 of table 12.

for the first part (matrixupdate) of col. 6, we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} | s \cup \partial s | \leq | s | + | \partial s | \leq | \operatorname {s u p p} (v - \widetilde {v}) | + | \operatorname {s u p p} (w ^ {\text {a p p r}} - \widetilde {v}) | \\ \leq | \operatorname {s u p p} (v - \widetilde {v}) | + | \operatorname {s u p p} (w ^ {\text {a p p r}} - v) | + | \operatorname {s u p p} (v - \widetilde {v}) | \leq n ^ {a} + k + n ^ {a} \leq 3 k, \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first and the third steps both follow from triangle inequality, the second step follows from  $s = \operatorname{supp}(v - \widetilde{v})$  (part 5 assumption d.1) and  $\partial s = \operatorname{supp}(w^{\mathrm{app}} - \widetilde{v})$  (part 4 of definition d.2), the fourth step follows from col. 1 and col. 5 of table 12, the last step follows from the fact that  $k \geq n^{a}$  (fact f.10).

for the second part (partialmatrixupdate) of col. 6, we have

$$
| s \cup \partial s | \leq | s | + | \partial s | \leq | \operatorname {s u p p} (v - \widetilde {v}) | + | \operatorname {s u p p} (w ^ {\text {a p p r}} - \widetilde {v}) | \leq n ^ {a} + 2 n ^ {a} = 3 n ^ {a},
$$

where the first two steps are the same as previous inequality, and the third step follows from col. 2 and col. 5 of table 12.

for the fifth part (query) of col. 6, we have

$$
| s \cup \partial s | \leq | s | + | \partial s | \leq | \operatorname {s u p p} (v - \widetilde {v}) | + | \operatorname {s u p p} (w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}} - \widetilde {v}) | \leq n ^ {a} + n ^ {\widetilde {a}} \leq 2 n ^ {a},
$$

where the first two steps are the same as previous inequality, the third step follows from col. 2 and col. 5 of table 12.

# e.2 running time of query

the goal of this section is to prove lemma e.3. we will use the following sparsity guarantees that are proved in lemma e.1.

fact e.2 (sparsity guarantees for query). when entering query we have the following sparsity guarantee (from table 13):

1.  $\| \gamma +\partial \gamma \| _0\leq n^a$

4.  $\| \xi +\partial \xi \| _0\leq n^a$

7.  $|s|\leq n^{a}$

2.  $\| \partial \gamma \| _0\leq n^{\widetilde{a}}$

5.  $\| \partial \xi \| _0\leq n^{\widetilde{a}}$

3.  $\| \gamma \| _0\leq n^a$

6.  $|\partial s|\leq n^{\widetilde{a}}$

lemma e.3 (running time of query). in procedure query (algorithm 12), it takes

1.  $o(n^{1 + b} + n^{a + \tilde{a}})$  time to compute

$$
r _ {2} \leftarrow q [ j ] \xi + r [ j ] \gamma_ {2} + r [ j ] \partial \gamma m (\xi + \partial \xi) + \left(q [ j ] + r [ j ] \gamma m\right) \partial \xi ,
$$

2.  $o(n^{1 + b})$  time to compute

$$
r _ {3} \leftarrow r [ j ] (\gamma + \partial \gamma) \beta_ {2},
$$

3.  $o(n^{a + \bar{a}})$  time to compute

$$
\partial \gamma \gets b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (\beta_ {2}) _ {\partial s \backslash s} ] + b \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {r} [ (m _ {\partial s \backslash s}) ^ {\top} ] \cdot (\xi + \partial \xi) + e \cdot \partial \xi ,
$$

4.  $o(n^{a + \overline{a}})$  time to compute

$$
\left(u ^ {\prime}, c, u\right) \leftarrow \operatorname {d e c o m p o s e} \left(\mathcal {l} _ {*} \left[ \left(\delta_ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}, s ^ {\text {n e w}}}\right) ^ {- 1} + m _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}, s ^ {\text {n e w}}} \right] - \mathcal {l} _ {*} \left[ \delta_ {s, s} ^ {- 1} + m _ {s, s} \right]\right),
$$

5.  $o(\mathcal{t}_{\mathrm{mat}}(n^a, n^{\widetilde{a}}, n^{\widetilde{a}}))$  time to compute

$$
e ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \leftarrow e _ {\partial s} - b _ {(\partial s \backslash s)} m _ {(\partial s \backslash s), \partial s}, e _ {s ^ {\prime}} ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \leftarrow - e _ {s ^ {\prime}} ^ {\mathrm {t m p}}, e _ {(s \cap \partial s) \backslash s ^ {\prime}} ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \leftarrow 0, a n d
$$

$$
u ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \leftarrow [ b _ {\partial s}, b _ {\partial s}, e ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} ],
$$

6.  $o(\mathcal{t}_{\mathrm{mat}}(n^{\widetilde{a}},n^{a},n^{\widetilde{a}}))$  time to compute

$$
\gamma^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \gets u ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} (c ^ {- 1} + u ^ {\top} u ^ {\mathrm {t m p}}) ^ {- 1} u ^ {\top} \cdot (\gamma_ {1} + \partial \gamma),
$$

7.  $o(n^{\widetilde{a} + a} + n^{1 + b})$  time to compute

$$
r _ {4} \gets \left(\mathcal {l} _ {c} [ (q [ j ]) _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} ] + f [ j ] + r [ j ] \gamma \cdot (\mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {\partial s \setminus s} ] - \mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {s ^ {\prime}} ]) + r [ j ] \partial \gamma \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} ]\right) \cdot (\gamma^ {\mathrm {t m p}} - \gamma_ {1} - \partial \gamma).
$$

overall, the time to compute  $r$  (which is  $r_1 + r_2 + r_3 + r_4$ ) is

$$
o (n ^ {1 + b} + \mathcal {t} _ {\mathrm {m a t}} (n ^ {\widetilde {a}}, n ^ {a}, n ^ {\widetilde {a}})).
$$

claim e.4 (part 1 of lemma e.3). in procedure query (algorithm 12), it takes  $o(n^{1 + b} + n^{a + \widetilde{a}})$  time to compute

$$
r _ {2} \leftarrow q [ j ] \xi + r [ j ] \gamma_ {2} + \underbrace {r [ j ] \partial \gamma m (\xi + \partial \xi)} _ {a _ {1}} + \underbrace {\left(q [ j ] + r [ j ] \gamma m\right) \partial \xi} _ {a _ {2}}.
$$

proof. the running time of this step can be split into the following parts:

the first part is to compute  $q[j]\xi$  by multiplying a  $n^b \times n$  matrix  $q[j]$  with a  $n \times 1$  vector  $\xi$ . it takes  $o(n^{1 + b})$  time. the second part is to compute  $r[j]\gamma_{2}$  by multiplying a  $n^b \times n$  matrix  $r[j]$  with a  $n \times 1$  vector  $\gamma_{2}$ . it takes  $o(n^{1 + b})$  time.

the third part is to compute  $a_1$  as follows:

1. we multiply a  $n^{\widetilde{a}}$ -sparse  $n \times n$  diagonal matrix  $\partial \gamma$  (part 2 of fact e.2) with a  $n \times n$  matrix  $m$  and then with a  $n^a$ -sparse  $n \times 1$  vector  $(\xi + \partial \xi)$  (part 4 of fact e.2). it takes  $o(n^{a + \widetilde{a}})$  time.

2. we multiply a  $n^b \times n$  matrix  $r[j]$  and a  $n \times 1$  vector  $(\partial \gamma m(\xi + \partial \xi))$ . it takes  $o(n^{1 + b})$  time.

so computing  $a_1$  takes  $o(n^{a + \tilde{a}} + n^{1 + b})$  time in total.

the fourth part is to compute  $a_2$  as follows:

1. we multiply a  $n^a$ -sparse  $n \times n$  diagonal matrix  $\gamma$  (part 3 of fact e.2) with a  $n \times n$  matrix  $m$  and then with a  $n^{\widetilde{a}}$ -sparse  $n \times 1$  vector  $\partial \xi$  (part 5 of fact e.2). it takes  $o(n^{a + \widetilde{a}})$  time.  
2. we multiply a  $n^b \times n$  matrix  $r[j]$  with a  $n \times 1$  vector  $(\gamma m \partial \xi)$ . it takes  $o(n^{1 + b})$  time.  
3. we multiply a  $n^b \times n$  matrix  $q[j]$  and a  $n^{\widetilde{a}}$ -sparse  $n \times 1$  vector  $\partial \xi$  (part 5 of fact e.2). it takes  $o(n^{\widetilde{a} + b})$  time.

so computing  $a_2$  takes  $o(n^{a + \tilde{a}} + n^{1 + b})$  time in total since  $\widetilde{a} \leq 1$ .

thus the total running time is  $o(n^{1 + b} + n^{a + \tilde{a}})$

claim e.5 (part 2 of lemma e.3). in procedure query (algorithm 12), it takes  $o(n^{1 + b})$  time to compute  $r_3 \gets r[j](\gamma + \partial \gamma)\beta_2$ .

proof. the running time of this step can be split into the following parts.

1. we multiply a  $n^a$ -sparse  $n \times n$  diagonal matrix  $(\gamma + \partial \gamma)$  (part 1 of fact e.2) and a  $n \times 1$  vector  $\beta_2$ . it takes  $o(n^a)$  time.  
2. we multiply a  $n^b \times n$  matrix  $r[j]$  and a  $n \times 1$  vector  $((\gamma + \partial \gamma)\beta_2)$ . it takes  $o(n^{1 + b})$  time.

the total running time is  $o(n^{a} + n^{1 + b}) = o(n^{1 + b})$  since  $a \leq 1$ .

claim e.6 (part 3 of lemma e.3). in procedure query (algorithm 12), it takes  $o(n^{a + \tilde{a}})$  time to compute  $\partial \gamma \gets b\cdot \mathcal{l}_r[(\beta_2)_{\partial s\backslash s}] + b\cdot \mathcal{l}_r[(m_{\partial s\backslash s})^\top ]\cdot (\xi +\partial \xi) + e\cdot \partial \xi$

proof. the running time of this step can be split into the following parts.

1. we multiply a  $6n^{a} \times 6n^{a}$  matrix  $b$  with a  $n^{\widetilde{a}}$ -sparse  $6n^{a} \times 1$  vector  $\mathcal{l}_r[(\beta_2)_{\partial s \setminus s}]$  (since  $|\partial s \setminus s| \leq |\partial s| \leq n^{\widetilde{a}}$  by part 6 of fact e.2). it takes  $o(n^{a + \widetilde{a}})$  time.  
2. we multiply a  $6n^{a} \times n$  matrix  $\mathcal{l}_r[(m_{\partial s \setminus s})^\top]$  that only has  $n^{\widetilde{a}}$  non-zero rows (since  $|\partial s \setminus s| \leq |\partial s| \leq n^{\widetilde{a}}$  by part 6 of fact e.2) with a  $n^{a}$ -sparse  $n \times 1$  vector  $(\xi + \partial \xi)$  (part 4 of fact e.2). it takes  $o(n^{a + \widetilde{a}})$  time.  
3. we multiply a  $6n^{a} \times 6n^{a}$  matrix  $b$  with a  $n^{\widetilde{a}}$ -sparse  $6n^{a} \times 1$  vector  $(\mathcal{l}_r[(m_{\partial s \setminus s})^\top](\xi + \partial \xi))$  (since  $|\partial s \setminus s| \leq |\partial s| \leq n^{\widetilde{a}}$  by part 6 of fact e.2). it takes  $o(n^{a + \widetilde{a}})$  time.  
4. we multiply a  $6n^a \times n$  matrix  $e$  with a  $n^{\widetilde{a}}$ -sparse vector  $\partial \xi$  (part 5 of fact e.2). it takes  $o(n^{a + \widetilde{a}})$  time.

thus the total running time is  $o(n^{a + \widetilde{a}})$ .

claim e.7 (part 4 of lemma e.3). in procedure query (algorithm 12), it takes  $o(n^{a + \tilde{a}})$  time to compute

$$
\left(u ^ {\prime}, c, u\right) \leftarrow \operatorname {d e c o m p o s e} \left(\mathcal {l} _ {*} \left[ \left(\delta_ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}, s ^ {\text {n e w}}}\right) ^ {- 1} + m _ {s ^ {\text {n e w}}, s ^ {\text {n e w}}} \right] - \mathcal {l} _ {*} \left[ \delta_ {s, s} ^ {- 1} + m _ {s, s} \right]\right).
$$

and the size of the computed matrices are  $u', u \in \mathbb{r}^{n^a \times c}$ ,  $c \in \mathbb{r}^{c \times c}$ , where  $c \leq o(n^{\widetilde{a}})$ .

proof. for ease of notation, we denote  $n \coloneqq \mathcal{l}_{*}[(\delta_{s^{\mathrm{new}}, s^{\mathrm{new}}}^{\mathrm{new}})^{-1} + m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}, s^{\mathrm{new}}}] - \mathcal{l}_{*}[\delta_{s,s}^{-1} + m_{s,s}]$ .

from lemma c.5 we know that the non-zero entries of  $n$  can be split into three parts:  $n_{\partial s,(s\backslash \partial s)}$ ,  $n_{(s\backslash \partial s),\partial s}$ , and  $n_{\partial s,\partial s}$ . thus we don't need to compute  $n$  explicitly, but only compute the non-zero entries of  $n$ , which takes  $o(|\partial s|\cdot |s\backslash \partial s| + |\partial s|\cdot |\partial s|) = o(n^{a + \widetilde{a}})$  time (from part 7 and part 6 of fact e.2 and since  $\widetilde{a}\leq a$ ).

then  $n$  satisfies the requirement of lemma c.4 with  $s_{1} = s\backslash \partial s$ ,  $s_{2} = \partial s$ , so the function decompose can be computed in  $o(n^{a}|s_{2}|) = o(n^{a}|\partial s|) = o(n^{a + \widetilde{a}})$  time (part 6 of fact e.2). also using lemma c.4, we know that the computed matrix  $c$  has size  $c\times c = (3|s_2|)\times (3|s_2|) = (3|\partial s|)\times (3|\partial s|)$ , thus  $c\leq o(n^{\widetilde{a}})$  (part 6 of fact e.2).

thus the total running time is  $o(n^{a + \overline{a}})$ .

claim e.8 (part 5 of lemma e.3). in procedure query (algorithm 12), it takes  $o(\mathcal{t}_{\mathrm{mat}}(n^a, n^{\widetilde{a}}, n^{\widetilde{a}}))$  time to compute

$$
e ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \gets e _ {\partial s} - b _ {(\partial s \cap s)} m _ {(\partial s \cap s), \partial s}; e _ {s ^ {\prime}} ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \gets - e _ {s ^ {\prime}} ^ {\mathrm {t m p}}; e _ {(s \cap \partial s) \backslash s ^ {\prime}} ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \gets 0; u ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \gets [ b _ {\partial s}, b _ {\partial s}, e ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} ].
$$

proof. to compute the initial  $e^{\mathrm{tmp}} \gets e_{\partial s} - b_{(\partial s \cap s)} m_{(\partial s \cap s), \partial s}$ , we need to multiply a  $6n^{a} \times |\partial s \cap s|$  matrix  $b_{(\partial s \cap s)}$  with a  $|\partial s \cap s| \times |\partial s|$  matrix  $m_{(\partial s \cap s), \partial s}$ , and this takes  $\mathcal{t}_{\mathrm{mat}}(6n^{a}, |\partial s \cap s|, |\partial s|) = o(\mathcal{t}_{\mathrm{mat}}(n^{a}, n^{\widetilde{a}}, n^{\widetilde{a}}))$  time (part 6 of fact e.2).

finally note that the other two steps  $e_{s'}^{\mathrm{tmp}} \gets -e_{s'}^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  and  $e_{(s \cap \partial s) \setminus s'}^{\mathrm{tmp}} \gets 0$  to adjust  $e^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  takes the same time as the size of  $e^{\mathrm{tmp}}$ , which is  $n^a \times |\partial s| = o(n^{a + \widetilde{a}})$  (part 6 of fact e.2). computing  $u^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  only needs to copy entries from the already computed  $b$  and  $e^{\mathrm{tmp}}$ , so it also takes the same time as the size of  $u^{\mathrm{tmp}}$ , which is  $n^a \times 3|\partial s| = o(n^{a + \widetilde{a}})$ . thus the total running time is

$$
o \left(\mathcal {t} _ {\mathrm {m a t}} \left(n ^ {a}, n ^ {\widetilde {a}}, n ^ {\widetilde {a}}\right) + n ^ {a + \widetilde {a}}\right) = o \left(\mathcal {t} _ {\mathrm {m a t}} \left(n ^ {a}, n ^ {\widetilde {a}}, n ^ {\widetilde {a}}\right)\right).
$$

claim e.9 (part 6 of lemma e.3). in procedure query (algorithm 12), it takes  $o(\mathcal{t}_{\mathrm{mat}}(n^{\widetilde{a}}, n^{a}, n^{\widetilde{a}}))$  time to compute

$$
\gamma^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \leftarrow u ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \underbrace {(c ^ {- 1} + u ^ {\top} u ^ {\mathrm {t m p}}) ^ {- 1}} _ {n} u ^ {\top} \cdot (\gamma_ {1} + \partial \gamma).
$$

proof. first note that from lemma e.7 we have that the sizes of  $u'$  and  $u$  are  $6n^a \times 3|\partial s|$ , and the size of  $c$  is  $3|\partial s| \times 3|\partial s|$ . from the assignment of  $u^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  on line 15, we know that the size of  $u^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  is also  $6n^a \times 3|\partial s|$ . the running time of this step can be split into the following parts:

the first part is to compute the  $3|\partial s| \times 3|\partial s|$  matrix  $n$ .

1. computing the inverse of a  $3|\partial s| \times 3|\partial s|$  matrix  $c$  takes  $o(n^{\widetilde{a}\omega})$  time (part 6 of fact e.2).  
2. we multiply a  $3|\partial s| \times 6n^{a}$  rectangular matrix  $u^{\top}$  with a  $6n^{a} \times 3|\partial s|$  matrix  $u^{\mathrm{tmp}}$ , which takes  $\mathcal{t}_{\mathrm{mat}}(3|\partial s|, 6n^{a}, 3|\partial s|) = o(\mathcal{t}_{\mathrm{mat}}(n^{\widetilde{a}}, n^{a}, n^{\widetilde{a}}))$  time ( $|\partial s| \leq n^{\widetilde{a}}$  from part 6 of fact e.2).  
3. we add a  $3|\partial s| \times 3|\partial s|$  matrix  $c^{-1}$  with a  $3|\partial s| \times 3|\partial s|$  matrix  $u^{\top}u^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  and calculate its inverse. it takes  $o(|\partial s|^{\omega}) = o(n^{\tilde{a}\omega})$  time (part 6 of fact e.2).

thus in total computing  $n$  takes time  $o(\mathcal{t}_{\mathrm{mat}}(n^{\widetilde{a}},n^{a},n^{\widetilde{a}}) + n^{\widetilde{a}\omega}) = o(\mathcal{t}_{\mathrm{mat}}(n^{\widetilde{a}},n^{a},n^{\widetilde{a}}))$ , since  $\widetilde{a} \leq a$  and thus  $n^{\widetilde{a}\omega} = \mathcal{t}_{\mathrm{mat}}(n^{\widetilde{a}},n^{\widetilde{a}},n^{\widetilde{a}}) \leq \mathcal{t}_{\mathrm{mat}}(n^{\widetilde{a}},n^{a},n^{\widetilde{a}})$ .

the second part is to compute the  $6n^{a} \times 1$  vector  $\gamma^{\mathrm{tmp}}$ .

1. we multiply the  $3|\partial s| \times 6n^{a}$  matrix  $u^{\top}$  with a  $6n^{a} \times 1$  vector  $(\gamma_{1} + \partial \gamma)$ . this takes  $o(n^{a} \cdot |\partial s|) = o(n^{a + \widetilde{a}})$  time (part 6 of fact e.2).  
2. we multiply the  $3|\partial s| \times 3|\partial s|$  matrix  $n$  with a  $3|\partial s| \times 1$  vector  $u^{\top}(\gamma_{1} + \partial \gamma)$ . this takes  $o(|\partial s|^2) = o(n^{2\widetilde{a}})$  time (part 6 of fact e.2).  
3. we multiply the  $6n^{a} \times 3|\partial s|$  matrix  $u^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  with a  $3|\partial s| \times 1$  vector  $nu^{\top}(\gamma_{1} + \partial \gamma)$ . this takes  $o(n^{a} \cdot |\partial s|) = o(n^{a + \widetilde{a}})$  time (part 6 of fact e.2).

thus this part takes  $o(n^{a + \tilde{a}})$  time since  $\widetilde{a} \leq a$ .

thus the total time to compute  $\gamma^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  is

$$
o \left(\mathcal {t} _ {\operatorname {m a t}} \left(n ^ {\widetilde {a}}, n ^ {a}, n ^ {\widetilde {a}}\right) + n ^ {a + \widetilde {a}}\right) = o \left(\mathcal {t} _ {\operatorname {m a t}} \left(n ^ {\widetilde {a}}, n ^ {a}, n ^ {\widetilde {a}}\right)\right).
$$

![](images/bbe83bf1228fae9496a5c9751c24d059e466bc89f5dba97cdbbf684b37533bff.jpg)

claim e.10 (part 7 of lemma e.3). in procedure query (algorithm 12), it takes  $o(n^{\widetilde{a} + a} + n^{1 + b})$  time to compute

$$
r _ {4} \leftarrow \left(\mathcal {l} _ {c} [ (q [ j ]) _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} ] + f [ j ] + r [ j ] \gamma \cdot (\mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {\partial s \backslash s} ] - \mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {s ^ {\prime}} ]) + r [ j ] \partial \gamma \cdot \mathcal {l} _ {c} [ m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} ]\right) \cdot (\gamma^ {\mathrm {t m p}} - \gamma_ {1} - \partial \gamma).
$$

proof. the running time can be split into the following parts:

1. we multiply a  $n^{\widetilde{a}}$ -sparse  $n \times n$  diagonal matrix  $\partial \gamma$  (part 2 of fact e.2) with a  $n \times 6n^{a}$  matrix  $\mathcal{l}_c[m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}}$  with a  $6n^{a} \times 1$  vector  $(\gamma^{\mathrm{tmp}} - \gamma_{1} - \partial \gamma)$ . it takes  $o(n^{\widetilde{a} + a})$  time.  
2. we multiply a  $n^b \times n$  matrix  $r[j]$  with a  $n \times 1$  vector ( $\partial \gamma \mathcal{l}_c[m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}](\gamma^{\mathrm{tmp}} - \gamma_1 - \partial \gamma)$ ). it takes  $o(n^{1 + b})$  time.  
3. we multiply a  $n^a$ -sparse  $n \times n$  diagonal matrix  $\gamma$  (part 3 of fact e.2) with a  $n \times 6n^a$  matrix  $\mathcal{l}_c[m_{\partial s \setminus s}]$  that only has  $n^{\widetilde{a}}$  non-zero columns (since  $|\partial s \setminus s| \leq |\partial s| \leq n^{\widetilde{a}}$  by part 6 of fact e.2) and then with a  $6n^a \times 1$  vector  $(\gamma^{\mathrm{tmp}} - \gamma_1 - \partial \gamma)$ . it takes  $o(n^{a + \widetilde{a}})$  time.  
4. we multiply a  $n^b \times n$  matrix  $r[j]$  with a  $n \times 1$  vector  $(\gamma(\mathcal{l}_c[m_{\partial s \setminus s}] - \mathcal{l}_c[m_{s'}])(\gamma^{\mathrm{tmp}} - \gamma_1 - \partial \gamma))$ . it takes  $o(n^{1 + b})$  time.  
5. we multiply a  $n^b \times 6n^a$  matrix  $f[j]$  with a  $6n^a \times 1$  vector  $(\gamma^{\mathrm{tmp}} - \gamma_1 - \partial \gamma)$ . it takes  $o(n^{1 + b})$  time.  
6. we multiply a  $n^b \times 6n^a$  matrix  $\mathcal{l}_c[(q[j])_{s^{\mathrm{new}}}]$  with a  $6n^a \times 1$  vector  $(\gamma^{\mathrm{tmp}} - \gamma_1 - \partial \gamma)$ . it takes  $o(n^{b + a})$  time.

thus this part takes  $o(n^{\widetilde{a} + a} + n^{1 + b} + n^{b + a}) = o(n^{\widetilde{a} + a} + n^{1 + b})$  time, since  $a \leq 1$ .

thus, the total running time to compute  $r_4$  is  $o(n^{\widetilde{a} + a} + n^{1 + b})$ .

![](images/f6431a062ad2b9387647523f0d3c8729dfb95171acfab053549b8ce48aa91c19.jpg)

proof of lemma e.3. summing over the time to compute  $r_2, r_3, (u', c, u), \partial \gamma, r_{4,1}, r_{4,2}, r_{4,3}, u^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  and  $r_{4,4}$ , the total running time of computing  $r$  is

$$
o \left(n ^ {1 + b} + n ^ {a + \widetilde {a}} + n ^ {a + b} + \mathcal {t} _ {\operatorname {m a t}} \left(n ^ {\widetilde {a}}, n ^ {a}, n ^ {\widetilde {a}}\right)\right) = o \left(n ^ {1 + b} + \mathcal {t} _ {\operatorname {m a t}} \left(n ^ {\widetilde {a}}, n ^ {a}, n ^ {\widetilde {a}}\right)\right),
$$

which follows by  $a \leq 1$  and  $n^{a + \widetilde{a}} \leq \mathcal{t}_{\mathrm{mat}}(n^{\widetilde{a}}, n^{a}, n^{\widetilde{a}})$ .

# e.3 running time of matrixupdate

the goal of this section is to prove lemma e.12. we will use the following sparsity guarantees that are proved in lemma e.1.

fact e.11 (sparsity guarantees for matrixupdate). when entering matrixupdate we have the following sparsity guarantee (from table 13):

1.  $|s^{\mathrm{new}}| \leq |s \cup \partial s| \leq 3k$ ,

2.  $\| \gamma^{\mathrm{new}}\| _0 = \| \gamma +\partial \gamma \| _0 = k$

lemma e.12 (running time of matrixupdate). in the procedure matrixupdate (in algorithm 13) it takes

1.  $o(k^{\omega + o(1)} + \mathcal{t}_{\mathrm{mat}}(n, k, n))$  time to compute

$$
m ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \leftarrow m - m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} \cdot \left(\left(\delta_ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}, s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}\right) ^ {- 1} + m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}, s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}}\right) ^ {- 1} \left(m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}}\right) ^ {\top},
$$

2.  $o(k^{\omega + o(1)} + \mathcal{t}_{\mathrm{mat}}(n^{1 + o(1)}, k, n))$  time to compute

$$
q ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \gets q + r (\gamma^ {\mathrm {n e w}} m ^ {\mathrm {t m p}}) + r \sqrt {v} (m ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} - m),
$$

3.  $o(n^{2 + o(1)})$  time to compute

$$
\beta_ {1} \gets q ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}),
$$

4.  $o(n^{2})$  time to compute

$$
\beta_ {2} \gets m ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \sqrt {w ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}} f (h ^ {\mathrm {a p p r}}).
$$

further, it takes  $o(n)$  time to do all other computation. so the overall running time of the procedure matrixupdate is  $o(\mathcal{t}_{\mathrm{mat}}(n^{1 + o(1)},k,n))$ .

claim e.13 (part 1 of lemma e.12). in procedure matrixupdate, it takes  $o(k^{\omega + o(1)} + \mathcal{t}_{\mathrm{mat}}(n, k, n))$  time to compute

$$
m ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \leftarrow m - m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} \cdot \left(\left(\delta_ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}, s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}} ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}\right) ^ {- 1} + m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}, s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}}\right) ^ {- 1} \left(m _ {s ^ {\mathrm {n e w}}}\right) ^ {\top}.
$$

proof. the running time of computing  $m^{\mathrm{new}}$  can be split into the following parts:

1. computing the inverse of a  $o(k) \times o(k)$  matrix  $((\delta_{s^{\mathrm{new}}, s^{\mathrm{new}}}^{\mathrm{new}})^{-1} + m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}, s^{\mathrm{new}}})$  (the size follows from  $|s^{\mathrm{new}}| = o(k)$ , see part 1 of fact e.11), this part takes  $o(k^{\omega + o(1)})$  time.  
2. computing the multiplication of a  $n \times o(k)$  matrix  $m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}}$  with a  $o(k) \times o(k)$  matrix  $((\delta_{s^{\mathrm{new}}s^{\mathrm{new}}})^{-1} + m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}s^{\mathrm{new}}})^{-1}$ , this part takes  $o(\mathcal{t}_{\mathrm{mat}}(n,k,k))$  time.  
3. computing the multiplication of a  $n \times o(k)$  matrix  $m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}}((\delta_{s^{\mathrm{new}}, s^{\mathrm{new}}}^{\mathrm{new}})^{-1} + m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}, s^{\mathrm{new}}})^{-1}$  with a  $o(k) \times n$  matrix  $(m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}})^\top$ , this part takes  $o(\mathcal{t}_{\mathrm{mat}}(n, k, n))$  time.  
4. subtracting a  $n \times n$  matrix  $m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}} \cdot ((\delta_{s^{\mathrm{new}}, s^{\mathrm{new}}}^{\mathrm{new}})^{-1} + m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}, s^{\mathrm{new}}})^{-1}(m_{s^{\mathrm{new}}})^\top$  from the  $n \times n$  matrix  $m$ , this part takes  $o(n^{2})$  time.

so in total computing  $m^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  takes time

$$
o (k ^ {\omega + o (1)} + \mathcal {t} _ {\mathrm {m a t}} (n, k, k) + \mathcal {t} _ {\mathrm {m a t}} (n, k, n) + n ^ {2}) = o (k ^ {\omega + o (1)} + \mathcal {t} _ {\mathrm {m a t}} (n, k, n)).
$$

claim e.14 (part 2 of lemma e.12). in matrixupdate, it takes  $o(k^{\omega + o(1)} + \mathcal{t}_{\mathrm{mat}}(n^{1 + o(1)}, k, n))$  time to compute

$$
q ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} \gets q + \underbrace {r (\gamma^ {\mathrm {n e w}} m ^ {\mathrm {t m p}})} _ {n _ {1}} + \underbrace {r \sqrt {v} (m ^ {\mathrm {t m p}} - m)} _ {n _ {2}}.
$$

proof. the running time of computing  $q^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  can be split into the following parts:

the first part is to compute the  $n\times n$  matrix  $n_{1}$

1. multiplying a  $k$ -sparse  $n \times n$  diagonal matrix  $\gamma^{\mathrm{new}}$  (part 2 of fact e.11) with a  $n \times n$  matrix  $m^{\mathrm{tmp}}$  takes  $o(kn)$  time.
